2026-11-15T15:21:41.0822247Z [36;1m  recover_result() {[0m

2026-11-15T15:21:41.0828524Z [36;1m        else[0m

2026-11-15T15:21:41.0833569Z [36;1m        sleep 1[0m
2026-11-15T15:21:41.0833916Z [36;1m        continue[0m
2026-11-15T15:21:41.0834224Z [36;1m      fi[0m

2026-11-15T15:21:41.0835788Z [36;1m        return[0m
2026-11-15T15:21:41.0836112Z [36;1m      fi[0m
2026-11-15T15:21:41.0836441Z [36;1m      if [ "${#previous[@]}" -gt 0 ]; then[0m
2026-11-15T15:21:41.0837213Z [36;1m        echo "::error::Original AOP leader ended without a completion result."[0m
2026-11-15T15:21:41.0838223Z [36;1m        exit 1[0m
2026-11-15T15:21:41.0838929Z [36;1m      fi[0m

2026-11-15T15:21:41.0840845Z [36;1m    echo "::error::Unable to retrieve stable AOP leader logs."[0m
2026-11-15T15:21:41.0841768Z [36;1m    exit 1[0m
2026-11-15T15:21:41.0842333Z [36;1m  }[0m
